Abstract: Affective computing systems are increasingly embedded in pervasive and interactive environments, such as adaptive games, assistive technologies, and resource-constrained platforms, where computational efficiency must be balanced with reliability across diverse users. Model pruning offers an effective way to reduce computational demands, yet existing approaches typically optimise for sparsity alone, without accounting for how parameter removal impacts robustness across individuals. In this work, we introduce Variance-Regularised Pruning (VR), a pruning framework that explicitly incorporates cross-participant stability into the sparsification process. Rather than relying solely on average prediction error, VR evaluates each connection based on its joint contribution to both prediction accuracy and variability across users, prioritising parameters that remain reliable under distributional differences. We evaluate the proposed approach on the AGAIN dataset, which includes arousal annotations collected across nine affect-eliciting game environments. Experimental results demonstrate that VR maintains competitive Concordance Correlation Coefficient (CCC) performance even at 80\% sparsity without additional fine-tuning, highlighting its suitability for deployment in real-world, resource-limited affect-aware systems. Overall, the proposed framework supports the development of compact, robust affective models that can operate reliably in real-world interactive environments.

</details>


### [194] [Energy-Structured Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27482)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Qi Tian,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E²-LoRA方法，通过能量集中与排序的低秩适配，解决持续学习中正交子空间方法的知识扩散和容量耗尽问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交子空间方法在持续学习中面临知识能量扩散问题，导致知识压缩效率低、任务容量快速耗尽。作者观察到参数更新引起的输出特征漂移本质上是低秩的，从而启发利用低秩结构来优化。

Method: 1) 理论证明沿特征漂移主方向保留参数能最小化输出重构误差；2) 提出E²-LoRA，将知识显式排序并集中到前k个秩上；3) 设计动态秩分配策略，联合优化能量保留与模型可塑性，平衡稳定性与可塑性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到state-of-the-art性能，有效释放了后续任务的学习容量。

Conclusion: E²-LoRA通过能量集中与排序有效解决了持续学习中的知识扩散和容量问题，验证了低秩适配在增量学习中的有效性。

Abstract: While orthogonal subspace methods try to mitigate task interference in Continual Learning (CL), they often suffer from energy diffusion across the basis, hindering knowledge compaction and exhausting capacity for future tasks. We observe that output feature drift induced by parameter updates is inherently low-rank, and theoretically prove that preserving parameters along the principal directions of this drift minimizes the output reconstruction error. Motivated by this, we propose \textbf{E}nergy-Concentrated and \textbf{E}nergy-Ordered \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank \textbf{A}daptation (E$^2$-LoRA). By explicitly ordering and concentrating knowledge into leading ranks, E$^2$-LoRA frees capacity for subsequent tasks. Furthermore, we design a dynamic rank allocation strategy to balance stability and plasticity by jointly optimizing energy retention and model plasticity. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that E$^2$-LoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [195] [Federated Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27486)
*Khayyam Nosrati,Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Olaf Sassnick,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习下多变量时间序列异常检测的数据集不足问题，提出了一个包含循环动态特性的新数据集，并评估了多种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集无法同时满足规模大、标签准确和无缺陷的要求，且离散工业自动化中常见的循环过程行为在MTSAD研究中未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个具有循环动态特性的数据集（源于离散自动化过程的重复性），并在该数据集和一个公开基准数据集上评估了多种MTSAD方法。

Result: 在所提出的循环动态数据集和公开基准数据集上对多种MTSAD方法进行了评估比较。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了FL范式下MTSAD的数据缺口，有助于更深入地理解循环过程行为在异常检测中的作用。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has broadened the horizon for multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD). However, benchmarking such anomaly detection methods within FL paradigm poses data-centric challenges. The existing datasets do not counteract these challenges since they do not simultaneously provide sufficient scale, accurate labels, and freedom from common flaws. In addition, the role of cyclic process behavior, which is common in discrete industrial automation, remains underexplored for MTSAD for the current state of research. This paper aims to shed more light on the literature and address these gaps by introducing a dataset designed with cyclic dynamics arising from the repetitive nature of discrete automation processes and evaluates selected MTSAD methods on both the proposed dataset and a public benchmark dataset.

</details>


### [196] [SparseOpt: Addressing Normalization-induced Gradient Skew in Sparse Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27541)
*Mohammed Adnan,Rohan Jain,Tom Jacobs,Ekansh Sharma,Rahul G. Krishnan,Rebekka Burkholz,Yani Ioannou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现批量归一化(BN)对动态稀疏训练(DST)有负面影响，并提出稀疏感知优化器SparseOpt，在保持稀疏性的同时实现更快收敛和更好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DST方法虽然能减少计算量，但收敛速度明显慢于密集训练，需要相近的训练时间才能达到相似精度，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验证明BN对稀疏训练的不利影响，提出SparseOpt（稀疏感知优化器）来解决该问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet数据集上，使用ResNet模型验证了SparseOpt实现了更快的收敛速度和更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统研究了批量归一化、稀疏层和DST之间的相互作用，为DST实际追赶密集训练性能迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Dynamic Sparse Training (DST) methods train neural networks by maintaining sparsity while dynamically adapting the network topology. Despite the promise of reduced computation, DST methods converge significantly slower than dense training, often requiring comparable training time to achieve similar accuracy. We demonstrate both analytically and empirically that Batch Normalization (BN) adversely affects sparse training, and propose SparseOpt, a sparsity-aware optimizer, to address this. Experiments on ResNet models across CIFAR-100 and ImageNet demonstrate consistently faster convergence and improved generalization with our proposed method. Our work highlights the limitations of current normalization layers in sparse training and provides the first systematic study of the interaction between Batch Normalization, sparse layers, and DST, taking a significant step toward making DST practically competitive with dense training.

</details>


### [197] [Information-theoretic Multimodal Representation Learning for Electrocardiogram Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27583)
*Phu X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Kontras,Wei Dai,Huy Phan,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Bert Vandenberk,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERIT是一个基于信息论的多模态心电图表征学习框架，通过联合掩码心电图建模和心电图-文本对比对齐，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，特别是在细粒度分类和零样本评估中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将心电图与临床报告对齐以引入诊断语义，但临床报告无法保留心电图波形中丰富的生理结构信息，尤其是在从粗粒度诊断类别到细粒度形态的多层次抽象上存在不足。

Method: 从信息论角度推导了一个联合保留信号结构与整合临床语义的可处理目标，基于此提出MERIT双分支预训练框架，包含掩码心电图建模和心电图-文本对比对齐两个组件。

Result: 在PTB-XL All上F1提升超过3%，SubClass分类提升超过5%；零样本评估中AUC提升+2.66%，F1提升+2.11%；且在多种分布偏移场景下具有鲁棒性；用于条件临床文本生成时在ROUGE和METEOR等指标上也有提升。

Conclusion: MERIT能够学习更具信息量和临床意义的心电图表征，特别是在细粒度临床应用方面表现突出。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used non-invasive measurements of cardiac activity and play a central role in clinical diagnosis. Recent multimodal approaches align ECG signals with clinical reports to incorporate diagnostic semantics, but clinical reports often fail to preserve the rich physiological structure of ECG waveforms, particularly across multiple levels of abstraction ranging from coarse diagnostic categories to fine-grained morphology. To address this limitation, we formulate ECG representation learning from an information-theoretic perspective and derive a tractable objective that jointly preserves signal structure and integrates clinical semantics. Based on this principle, we propose \textbf{MERIT} (Multimodal ECG Representation via Information Theory), a dual-branch pretraining framework combining masked ECG modeling with ECG--text contrastive alignment. Extensive experiments on PTB-XL and additional benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over prior methods, including gains exceeding $3%$ F1 on PTB-XL All and $5%$ F1 on SubClass classification. In zero-shot evaluation, MERIT further improves performance by up to $ +2.66\%$ AUC and $ +2.11\%$ F1 on PTB-XL SubClass, while also demonstrating robustness under multiple distribution-shift settings. Moreover, leveraging the learned ECG representations for ECG-conditioned clinical text generation with large language models improves text quality across several metrics, including ROUGE and METEOR. Together, these results demonstrate that MERIT learns more informative and clinically meaningful ECG representations, particularly for fine-grained clinical applications.

</details>


### [198] [Gradient Transformer: Learning to Generate Updates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27591)
*Binh-Nguyen Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据无关的知识蒸馏框架Grad-Transformer，通过将微调后的小型语言模型（TinyLM）的更新向量转换为大型语言模型（LLM）的更新向量，使缺乏计算资源的组织无需共享私有数据即可更新LLM，并在多项任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织缺乏在私有数据上微调大型语言模型（LLM）的计算资源，而单独微调小型语言模型（TinyLM）效果较差，需要一种无需数据共享即可将TinyLM的微调效果迁移到LLM的方法。

Method: 提出Grad-Transformer框架，利用影子数据集学习TinyLM与LLM更新向量之间的关联，将TinyLM的更新向量转换为LLM的更新向量，支持多组织协作且无需访问私有数据。

Result: 在语言建模和推理任务上，Grad-Transformer显著优于现有最先进的知识蒸馏基线方法，并且在严格的差分隐私保护下仍保持优越性能。

Conclusion: Grad-Transformer框架能高效且低成本地将TinyLM的微调能力迁移至LLM，支持多方协作，同时保护数据隐私，为资源受限的组织提供了一种可行的LLM更新方案。

Abstract: Many organizations lack computational resources to fine-tune large language models (LLMs) on private (unshareable) data for better utility, while fine-tuning tiny language models (TinyLMs) alone performs poorly. To address this bottleneck, we propose a data-free knowledge distillation framework that generates LLM update vectors based on TinyLMs fine-tuned on private data. An update vector is a vector of parameter changes from an initial model to its fine-tuned version on a dataset, capturing the effect of cumulative gradient steps during fine-tuning. The key idea of our framework is a novel Gradient Transformer that transforms TinyLM's update vectors into LLM's update vectors. As derived from shadow datasets, Grad-Transformer captures the correlation between TinyLM and LLM update vectors, enabling third-party providers to generate LLM update vectors given the organization's TinyLM update vectors without accessing the organization's private data. The framework supports multi-organization collaboration to jointly update LLMs, improving performance and cost-efficiency. Extensive experiments across language modeling and reasoning tasks show that Grad-Transformer remarkably outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines, even under strict differential privacy protection.

</details>


### [199] [The Energy Blind Spot: NVIDIA's Flagship Edge AI Hardware Cannot Support Process-Level Energy Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27599)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC)平台上AI负载的能耗可观测性严重不足：仅支持GPU瞬时功耗测量，CPU能耗数据完全不可获取，并提出通过外部直流计量和GPU减法作为临时校准方案，呼吁将能耗可观测性作为硬件的一等公民需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI工作负载的编排结构主导了能耗成本（相比线性基线高达4.33-7.63倍），且CPU侧处理占据了高达90.6%的延迟和44%的动态能耗。然而，当前面向边缘部署的GB10桌面AI系统缺乏必要的CPU能耗监测能力，导致无法进行完整的能耗归因和优化。

Method: 对ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC)平台进行系统性能耗可观测性审计，检查支持的软件接口（CPU计数器、INA电源轨监视器、IPMI/BMC、SCMI powercap协议等），并发现MediaTek固件通过未文档化的ACPI接口（SPBM）内部计算每轨能耗，但NVIDIA明确表示"没有计划公开CPU轨信息"。

Result: 该平台仅支持通过NVML获取瞬时GPU功耗，无法通过任何支持的软件接口获取CPU能耗数据。提出了外部直流计量结合GPU减法的临时校准桥接方案，以及通过SCMI powercap的标准路径。

Conclusion: 在GB10平台上无法像x86通过RAPL那样进行设备端每进程能耗归因，必须通过外部测量方式弥补。呼吁低碳计算社区将能耗可观测性作为硬件的一等公民需求。

Abstract: Agentic AI workloads - where a single user goal triggers multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure recovery - are being targeted for edge deployment, with NVIDIA, Dell, HP, ASUS, MSI, Acer, and Gigabyte all shipping GB10-based desktop AI systems in 2026. We recently demonstrated that orchestration structure dominates agentic energy cost, with workflows consuming 4.33x more energy per successful goal than linear baselines and OOI reaching 7.63x for multi-step reasoning tasks. Separately, Rajat et al. show that CPU-side processing accounts for up to 90.6% of total latency and 44% of total dynamic energy in agentic workloads. We report a systematic energy-observability audit of the ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC) and find that the platform exposes no CPU energy counter, no INA power-rail monitor, no IPMI/BMC, and no SCMI powercap protocol through any supported software interface. The only on-device energy telemetry is instantaneous GPU power via NVML. We further discover that the MediaTek firmware already computes per-rail energy internally via an undocumented ACPI interface (SPBM), but NVIDIA states there are "no plans to expose CPU rail information." On-device per-process energy attribution - as performed on x86 via RAPL - is therefore not reproducible on this platform through supported interfaces. We formalize a hardware requirements specification for energy-attributed AI, propose an interim calibration bridge using external DC metering combined with GPU subtraction, and identify a standards-track path via SCMI powercap. Our findings motivate the low-carbon computing community to demand energy observability as a first-class hardware requirement.

</details>


### [200] [Evaluating Local Explainability Metrics for Machine Learning Models on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27618)
*Tomás Pereira,João Vitorino,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LIME、SHAP和Feature Ablation等局部可解释性技术在复杂表格分类任务中的可信度，发现解释质量主要受数据集复杂性和特征分布影响，而非模型预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性技术生成的解释可能看似合理但未能捕捉模型内部推理，尤其在处理复杂表格数据时可靠性存疑，因此需要系统评估其可信度。

Method: 在32个数据集上对LIME、Kernel SHAP和Feature Ablation三种技术进行基准测试，从忠实性、鲁棒性和复杂性三个维度评估，并划分共识正确和共识错误两组样本进行分析。

Result: 解释质量与模型预测性能并非总是相关；数据集复杂性和特征分布是影响解释质量和可靠性的主要因素。

Conclusion: 局部可解释性技术的可信度受数据集特性影响更大，而非模型本身的表现，需谨慎使用这些解释方法。

Abstract: Despite the wide use of explainability techniques to attempt to understand the behavior of Artificial Intelligence (AI), the generated explanations may not always be reliable. An explanation can appear plausible to humans but fail to capture the internal reasoning of a model, particularly when dealing with complex tabular data. This paper studies the trustworthiness of local explainability techniques when applied to complex tabular classification tasks, considering evaluated metrics for three main properties: faithfulness to the model's predictions, robustness to input data variations, and complexity of the explanation itself. A benchmark was performed for Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME), Kernel SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), and Feature Ablation techniques, across 32 datasets and different types of machine learning models. Model performance ranges were analyzed to identify two groups: consensus-correct, which are samples that all models predicted correctly, and consensus-wrong, samples that all models predicted incorrectly. The obtained results demonstrate that that the explanations are not always correlated with a model's predictive performance. Instead, dataset complexity and feature distributions seem to be the main factors affecting explanation quality and reliability.

</details>


### [201] [Supervised Distributional Reduction via Optimal Transport and Dependence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27619)
*Sai-Aakash Ramesh,Archit Sood,Andrew Corbett,Tim Dodwell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督分布约简（SDR）算法，通过融合最优传输与显式依赖最大化，学习既能捕捉数据几何结构又能保留目标任务相关信号的紧凑表示，并利用其诱导的非平稳几何构建自适应高斯过程核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布约简方法在监督场景下探索不足，难以在数据压缩与预测保真度之间取得平衡，且缺乏能同时保留几何结构与任务相关信号的表示学习方法。

Method: 基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein目标对齐输入分布与代表点，并引入直接依赖项最大化嵌入与目标变量的相关性，实现监督信号显式融入；再利用学习到的表示定义自适应距离，构建非平稳高斯过程核。

Result: 学习到的紧凑表示同时反映几何结构与监督信息，且诱导的数据依赖非平稳几何可有效用于高斯过程建模，提供了一种基于最优传输的非平稳核设计新视角。

Conclusion: SDR通过显式依赖最大化将监督信号融入分布约简，实现了更优的预测保真度，并为非平稳核设计提供了最优传输框架下的理论支撑。

Abstract: Learning representations that capture both intrinsic data geometry and target-relevant structure remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in settings where data reduction must balance compression with predictive fidelity. While distributional reduction-encompassing joint clustering and dimensionality reduction-offers a principled way to summarize data, its supervised variants remain relatively under-explored, despite the importance of retaining task-relevant signal for downstream prediction and decision-making. We propose Supervised Distributional Reduction (SDR), an algorithm for learning target-aware representations by combining optimal transport with explicit dependence maximization. SDR builds on the Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) objective to align the relational structure of the input distribution with a set of representative points, while augmenting it with a direct dependence term that encourages the learned embeddings to capture predictive signal more explicitly. This results in compact representations that reflect both geometric structure and supervision. Beyond representation learning, SDR naturally induces a data-dependent, non-stationary geometry that can be leveraged for settings such as Gaussian Process (GP) modelling. By redefining distances through target-aware distributional alignment, SDR enables the construction of adaptive kernels that respond to local variations in both data geometry and supervision, offering an optimal transport-based perspective on non-stationary kernel design.

</details>


### [202] [Transferable Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Latent Embeddings and Dynamic Policy Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27659)
*Gengyue Han,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过概率潜嵌入和动态策略适应的强化学习框架，用于解决Sim2Real迁移中的安全与效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法（如鲁棒安全强化学习和域随机化）在处理未建模系统动态时，存在性能下降或残留安全风险的问题。

Method: 基于元强化学习的潜上下文变量推断环境表示，结合分布强化学习公式，根据潜变量估计精度动态调整策略的风险水平。

Result: 在部署早期提升安全性，并通过快速策略适应提高Sim2Real迁移效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效应对Sim2Real差距，实现安全且高效的策略迁移。

Abstract: Due to limited resources and public safety concerns, deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents for many cyber-physical systems (e.g., autonomous vehicles) are first trained in simulators. However, when deployed in real world environments, they often suffer from performance degradation or safety violations because of the inevitable Sim2Real gap. Existing zero-shot approaches, such as robust safe RL and domain randomization, mitigate this issue but typically at the cost of degraded performance or residual safety risks when experiencing unmodeled system dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework that enables safe and efficient policy transfer via probabilistic latent embeddings and dynamic policy adaptation. We consider a family of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) under different environment contexts. By leveraging latent context variable in meta-RL, the proposed framework infers the latent representation of the environment from simulated experiences. Furthermore, it incorporates a distributional RL formulation, which allows risk levels of the deployed policy to be adjusted dynamically, based on the estimation accuracy of the latent context variable. This strategy promotes safety at the early deployment stage and improves efficiency through fast policy adaptation under the Sim2Real gap.

</details>


### [203] [Aligning LLMs with Human Uncertainty: A Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator for LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27668)
*Hui Dai,Ryan Teehan,Parsa Torabian,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，利用二元结果和人类预测两种信号，将点估计转换为事件可能性的分布，实现更优的校准和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测方法通常仅从二元结果学习输出言语化预测，未能充分利用人类预测中蕴含的群体概率估计和预测者共识程度等丰富信息。

Method: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，将模型初始点估计转换为事件可能性的分布：事件似然p服从Beta分布，结果y服从Bernoulli分布，均值作为校准后的点预测，方差作为认知不确定性。

Result: BBC在校准和准确性上均优于传统事后校准方法和专门微调的预测模型，且保持轻量级和良好的泛化能力；BBC捕获的认知不确定性比言语化置信度更能可靠预测预测误差。

Conclusion: 利用人类预测和二元结果的双重监督信号，通过贝叶斯建模可有效提升LLM概率预测的校准性能和不确定性估计质量。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting estimates the likelihood of uncertain future events. To improve LLM forecasting, existing methods typically learn from binary outcomes to output verbalized forecasts. However, while aggregated human forecasts contain rich information in both the crowd probability estimate and the degree of agreement among forecasters, how to utilize these signals remains underexplored. To address this, we propose the Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC), which converts an initial point estimate forecast from any model into a distribution over event likelihood, using supervision from both binary outcomes and human forecasts. BBC models event likelihood $p \sim \text{Beta}(α, β)$ and outcome $y \sim \text{Bernoulli}(p)$, with the mean as the calibrated point forecast and the variance as the epistemic uncertainty. Our results show that BBC generally provides better calibrated and more accurate forecasts than both traditional post-hoc calibration methods and models fine-tuned specifically for forecasting, while remaining lightweight and having good generalization. We also show that the epistemic uncertainty captured by BBC is a more reliable predictor of forecasting error than verbalized confidence.

</details>


### [204] [Heterogeneous Parallelism for Multimodal Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27678)
*Yashaswi Karnati,Kamran Jafari,Akash Mehra,Li Ding,Pranav Prashant Thombre,Ali Roshan Ghias,Shifang Xu,Parth Mannan,Yu Yao,Hao Wu,Eric Harper,Ashwath Aithal,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构并行训练范式，允许多模态大模型中的不同模块独立设置并行布局和位置，通过边界通信器解决张量语义转换问题，显著提升训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单一LLM中心化TP/CP/PP/DP/EP并行布局迫使编码器继承LLM驱动的分片和放置策略，导致通信开销增加、编码器并行受限以及LLM调度受限，尤其在长上下文场景中问题更为突出。

Method: 提出异构并行抽象，使端到端图中的不同模块可使用独立布局和秩放置；设计边界通信器实现前向和反向布局变换；扩展调度支持共置和非共置两种放置模式。

Result: 共置异构并行提升TFLOPS/GPU最高49.3%，非共置异构并行提升聚合token吞吐量最高13.0%和TFLOPS/GPU最高9.6%。

Conclusion: 异构并行打破了LLM-centric布局的耦合限制，在多模态和长上下文训练中找到了更优的操作点，且损失收敛性与同构基线持平，系统已作为Megatron-LM扩展开源。

Abstract: Foundation model training is becoming multimodal, from post-training pipelines to large-scale pretraining. As modality coverage broadens, context windows grow, and encoder LLM scales diverge, a single LLM-centric TP/CP/PP/DP/EP layout increasingly limits throughput. This coupling forces encoders to inherit LLM-driven sharding and placement choices that can add communication, limit encoder parallelism, or constrain the LLM schedule; the mismatch is most pronounced at long contexts, where LLM context parallelism is needed for the fused multimodal sequence but encoder inputs remain bounded. We present heterogeneous parallelism for multimodal large language model training, an abstraction that lets modules in one end-to-end graph use independent layouts and rank placements, supporting colocated execution on shared GPUs and non-colocated execution on disjoint rank sets. The key challenge is preserving boundary tensor semantics across independent layouts: forward activations must be materialized for the destination layout, while backward gradients must be routed back to the source layout. We address this with boundary communicators that implement forward and backward layout transforms, plus scheduling extensions for both placement modes. We evaluate optimized homogeneous, colocated heterogeneous, and non-colocated heterogeneous configurations across multimodal workloads and GPU scales to characterize when added layout and placement freedom exposes a better operating point. Across this sweep, colocated heterogeneity improves TFLOPS/GPU by up to 49.3%, while non-colocated heterogeneity improves aggregate token throughput by up to 13.0% and TFLOPS/GPU by up to 9.6%. We validate loss convergence parity against homogeneous baselines and release the system as an open-source Megatron-LM extension.

</details>


### [205] [Bayesian Deployment Approval for Learned Landing Controllers under Finite Rollout Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27720)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯批准框架，用于在有限验证数据下评估学习型自主着陆控制器的部署准备度，通过后验推断量化不确定性，相较于传统累计奖励和成功率指标提供了更校准的部署风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习评估指标（累计奖励、经验成功率）在有限仿真轨迹下无法提供充分的统计证据来判断控制器是否具备部署条件，容易产生过度自信的部署解释。

Method: 提出概率着陆能力公式，基于触地安全性满足不确定运行条件；采用贝叶斯后验推断量化学习策略的真实部署能力不确定性；引入后验批准概率和后验部署风险；设计支持批准/拒绝/继续决策的序贯验证框架。

Result: 在PPO和SAC控制器的仿真实验中，传统经验成功率和奖励优化在有限验证证据下会产生过度自信的部署解释，而后验批准推断提供了更不确定性校准的部署准备度评估。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了传统强化学习评估与不确定性下部署导向验证之间的实用统计连接，并可推广至更广泛的学习型自主系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and data-driven autonomous controllers are commonly evaluated using cumulative reward and empirical success frequency under finite simulation trajectories. However, such empirical metrics do not necessarily provide sufficient statistical evidence regarding deployment readiness under uncertainty. This work develops a Bayesian approval framework for learned autonomous landing controllers under finite rollout evidence. A probabilistic landing capability formulation is introduced based on touchdown safety satisfaction under uncertain operating conditions, while Bayesian posterior inference is used to quantify uncertainty regarding the true deployment capability of learned policies. Posterior approval probability and posterior deployment risk are further introduced for deployment-oriented evaluation, together with a sequential validation framework supporting approve/reject/continue decisions during progressive rollout testing. Simulation experiments using PPO and SAC controllers demonstrate that empirical success and reward optimization may produce overconfident deployment interpretation under limited validation evidence, whereas posterior approval inference provides a more uncertainty-calibrated assessment of deployment readiness. The proposed framework provides a practical statistical connection between conventional reinforcement-learning evaluation and deployment-oriented validation under uncertainty and may be generalized to broader classes of learned autonomous systems.

</details>


### [206] [A Paired Testing Protocol for Batch-Conditioned Refusal Robustness in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27763)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 批处理条件（batch condition）可能改变语言模型的安全标签，但影响率极低（0.16%真实翻转），且通过VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1内核消融可完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估常将服务配置视为固定背景，但同一提示词在单独、同步批处理或连续批处理调度器下可能产生不同结果，批处理条件是一个未被测试的变量。

Method: 四项研究：A-本地发现+评分员校正+真实批处理确认；B-跨模型泛化测试；C-连续批处理组合测试；D-批处理不变内核消融。采用配对测试协议。

Result: 安全标签变化率0.51%（vs能力标签0.14%），校正后真实翻转率0.16%；15模型测试中无通用安全偏向（翻转率0.94x），输出不稳定性是最强脆弱性筛查指标（r=0.909）；内核消融使翻转从22/55降至0/55。

Conclusion: 建议进行精确堆栈验证：在服务批处理设置下评估拒绝行为，安全提示与能力控制配对，分别报告低率方向翻转与聚合零效应。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of language models often treat serving configuration as fixed background infrastructure, but batch condition is an untested treatment variable whenever the same prompt may be evaluated alone, in a synchronized batch, or inside a continuous-batching scheduler. We synthesize four artifact-backed studies into a paired testing protocol: Study A combines local discovery, scorer-corrected adjudication, and true-batching confirmation; Study B tests cross-model generalization; Study C tests continuous-batch composition; and Study D runs a batch-invariant-kernel ablation. The local test finds safety-label changes more often than capability-label changes (0.51% vs. 0.14%), but adjudication of 63 candidate rows leaves only 17 genuine behavioral flips, implying a corrected full-set rate of 0.16%. The 15-model extension finds no detectable universal safety-over-capability skew: flips are near parity (0.94x), alignment type has no detectable association ($p=0.942$, $η^2=0.033$), and output instability is the strongest tested fragility screen ($r=0.909$, bootstrap 95% CI [0.65, 0.97]). In the targeted kernel ablation, standard vLLM reproduces 22/55 label flips on current score-flip candidates, while enabling VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1 reduces the same test to 0/55 flips; the composition test separately finds no aggregate effect at 4.7pp sensitivity. The testing recommendation is exact-stack validation: evaluate refusal at the served batch setting, pair safety prompts with capability controls, and report low-rate directional flips separately from aggregate null effects.

</details>


### [207] [Restoring the Sweet Spot: Pass-Rate Weighted Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27765)
*Zehao Liu,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SC-SDPO，通过对SDPO的损失按 $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ 加权，引入隐式课程学习，在科学推理和工具使用基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO使用KL散度作为优势函数，但缺乏GRPO中基于组相对优势的隐式难度感知能力，导致无法自动聚焦于中等难度问题，影响学习效率。

Method: 从GRPO的优势归一化角度分析，发现归一化吸收了方差项 $p(1-p)$，使梯度中残留 $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ 作为缩放因子。据此提出SC-SDPO，对每个问题的SDPO损失乘以 $[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$ 权重，该权重可通过on-policy rollout的批自适应归一化零成本获得。

Result: 在科学推理和工具使用基准上，SC-SDPO相比SDPO在Qwen3-8B上提升+3.2/+4.3（mean@16/maj@16），在OLMo-3-7B上提升+1.8/+3.0，且训练动态稳定。

Conclusion: 通过简单的权重调整，SC-SDPO有效弥补了SDPO缺乏难度感知的缺陷，以零成本引入隐式课程学习，显著提升了强化学习微调效果。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) provides dense token-level credit assignment for reinforcement learning with large language models by leveraging the model's own feedback-conditioned predictions as a self-teacher. Unlike GRPO, however, whose group-relative advantage naturally concentrates learning on a sweet spot of intermediate-difficulty questions, SDPO's KL-based advantage lacks an implicit notion of difficulty awareness.
  We analyze this gap through the lens of GRPO's advantage normalization. Extending the learnability framework to normalized rewards, we show that normalization absorbs the variance term $p(1-p)$, equalizing leading-order learnability across questions and leaving $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ as the sole residual scaling factor in the per-question gradient. This analysis yields a simple prescription: weight each question's SDPO loss by $[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$, resulting in SC-SDPO, a scale-consistent variant of SDPO.
  The proposed weights are obtained as a zero-cost byproduct of on-policy rollouts with batch-adaptive normalization, inducing an implicit curriculum that dynamically tracks the model's evolving competence. Experiments on scientific reasoning and tool-use benchmarks demonstrate that SC-SDPO consistently improves over SDPO, yielding gains of +3.2/+4.3 (mean@16/maj@16) on Qwen3-8B and +1.8/+3.0 on OLMo-3-7B, while preserving stable training dynamics throughout optimization.

</details>


### [208] [Fine-Tuning Dynamics of In-Context Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27774)
*Ruomin Huang,Eshaan Nichani,Jason D. Lee,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文学习如何利用模型参数中存储的事实知识，通过提出IC-recall任务并分析单层transformer的微调动态，证明模型能通过成对注意力模式成功完成任务，且只需极少量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论研究多聚焦于仅从提示中获取信息的上下文学习，但实际应用中模型需要从参数中检索事实知识。本文旨在形式化并分析这种基于事实知识回忆的上下文学习行为。

Method: 形式化定义了IC-recall任务，使用预构建的MLP关联记忆存储知识三元组，分析单层transformer在IC-recall数据上的监督微调动力学，并证明其收敛到特定的成对注意力模式。

Result: 模型成功执行IC-recall，微调所需样本数量仅为存储知识三元组数量的多对数级别。实验验证了理论预测，且成对注意力模式在MLP层被预训练而非预构建时仍会出现。

Conclusion: 本文为理解上下文学习如何结合参数化事实知识提供了理论框架，揭示了极少量样本即可实现知识回忆的机制。

Abstract: In-context learning \ -- performing tasks based on examples given in the prompt \ -- is an important capability that has emerged in large language models and has received significant attention in both theory and practice. Existing theoretical work often focuses on settings where the learning uses information purely from the prompt. However, many practical instances of in-context learning require the model to retrieve factual knowledge stored in the model's parameters, with the context serving to identify which knowledge is relevant. In this work, we study how in-context learning leverages factual knowledge recall. We formalize this behavior by introducing the \emph{in-context factual recall (IC-recall)} task, where a transformer is provided a context of (subject, answer) pairs generated from a hidden relation, along with a query subject, and must both infer this hidden relation and retrieve the corresponding answer. Factual knowledge is modeled by the transformer having access to a simple pre-constructed MLP associative memory storing (subject, relation, answer) triplets. We analyze the supervised fine-tuning dynamics of a one-layer transformer on IC-recall data and prove that the model successfully performs IC-recall by converging to a particular pairwise attention pattern. This fine-tuning stage requires a very small number of samples \ -- only polylogarithmic in the number of stored knowledge triplets. Experiments verify our theoretical predictions and show that the pairwise attention pattern emerges even when the MLP layer is pretrained instead of constructed.

</details>


### [209] [Revisiting ML Training under Fully Homomorphic Encryption: Convergence Guarantees, Differential Privacy, and Efficient Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27782)
*Yvonne Zhou,Mingyu Liang,Ivan Brugere,Danial Dervovic,Yue Guo,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Min Wu,Dana Dachman-Soled*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对全同态加密下的机器学习训练进行理论收敛性分析，并结合差分隐私与多项式近似方法，在提高计算效率的同时保障隐私安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHE下的机器学习训练缺乏理论收敛性分析，且标准DP-GD在加密场景下计算成本过高，亟需一种既高效又可证明收敛的隐私保护训练方法。

Method: 采用多项式近似激活函数和损失函数实现FHE兼容的近似梯度下降，在不依赖逐样本梯度裁剪的情况下集成差分隐私，并提出数据无关的超参数选择策略与多项式近似理论指导。

Result: 证明了所提方法的收敛性，计算效率优于标准DP-GD，同时实现可比的效用；提供了可独立应用的多项式近似策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为在敏感数据上实现高效、隐私且安全的机器学习训练奠定了理论基础，推进了FHE与DP结合的实际可行性。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical convergence analysis of machine learning training under fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), combined with a differentially private (DP) training algorithm tailored to encrypted computation. Our approach improves computational efficiency over standard differentially private gradient descent (DP-GD) while achieving comparable utility. In particular, we prove convergence of approximate gradient descent using polynomial approximations of activation and loss functions, which are required for FHE compatibility. To preserve privacy in downstream tasks, we integrate differential privacy without relying on costly per-sample gradient clipping, enabling scalable encrypted learning. We also provide data-independent hyperparameter selection and theoretically grounded strategies for polynomial approximation which can be of independent interest. Together, these contributions advance the feasibility of efficient, private, and secure machine learning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [210] [Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Youngrae Kim,Woosang Lim,YoungJin Heo,Minkyu Kim,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种训练无关的一次性深度剪枝方法LoRP，通过表征局部性指导层剪枝，在多种LLM族上提升困惑度和下游任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定冗余假设，缺乏对层间冗余架构差异性的考虑。

Method: 引入表征局部性分数（RLS），基于全局层间隐藏状态相似度，通过小校准集计算成对层相似性、聚类并按簇内冗余分配剪枝。

Result: 在多种LLM族上，剪枝后困惑度降低，下游任务准确率提升。

Conclusion: LoRP能有效识别不同架构中的层冗余模式，实现更优的一次性深度剪枝。

Abstract: Large language models are known to contain representational redundancy across network depth, making depth pruning an effective approach for improving inference efficiency. Existing one-shot pruning methods rely on local layer importance or fixed redundancy assumptions across architectures. We propose Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning (LoRP), a training-free one-shot depth pruning framework guided by representation locality. We show that inter-layer redundancy can be either localized or globally distributed depending on the LLM architecture. To characterize this phenomenon, we introduce Representation Locality Score (RLS), derived from global inter-layer hidden-state similarity. Using a small calibration set, LoRP computes pairwise layer similarity, clusters layers by representational similarity, and allocates pruning according to residual intra-cluster redundancy. Experiments across diverse LLM families show improvements in both perplexity and downstream task accuracy.

</details>


### [211] [Knowing When to Ask: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27788)
*Abhijit Kumar,Zoey Wu,Mohit Suley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARL方法，通过强化学习训练critic模型自主判断何时需要调用外部工具，在多个基准上提升准确率并减少不必要的工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型无法自主判断何时需要借助外部工具（如计算器），现有方法（提示工程、轨迹级强化学习）无法有效区分工具调用的必要性。

Method: CARL (Competence-Aware Reinforcement Learning)：在模型自身rollout上训练一个critic，通过在自然工具使用边界处分解轨迹，使每个片段获得独立信用分配，从而学习模型自身的能力边界。

Result: 7B模型在五个基准上平均提升6.7个EM点，3B模型提升9.7个EM点；Musique数据集上提升最大（7B +8.3 EM, 3B +9.0 EM）；在参数可回答的问题上减少53%工具调用；3B提升幅度是7B的1.4倍。

Conclusion: CARL能有效教会模型识别自身知识边界，显著提升工具调用决策质量，尤其受益于参数记忆容量较小的模型。

Abstract: Humans know when to reach for help e.g. $347 \times 28$ warrants a calculator while $2+2$ does not. Language models do not. Prompt-based approaches can instruct a model when to invoke tools, but this scaffolding does not teach it to recognize the boundary of its own knowledge. RL approaches that assign a single outcome reward to the whole trajectory fare no better: trajectory-level credit cannot isolate which tool call in a successful episode actually helped, nor penalize unnecessary calls. We propose \textbf{CARL} (\textbf{C}ompetence-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which trains a critic on the model's own rollouts to learn where parametric knowledge suffices and where it needs external help. By decomposing each rollout at natural tool-use boundaries (e.g., code fence delimiters and context block transitions), CARL assigns independent credit to each segment from a single binary outcome, without external judges or step-level annotations. As a result, erroneous tool calls, incorrect extractions, and unnecessary calls each receive appropriately signed advantages. The trained critic captures the model's domain competence: it separates parametrically solvable from tool-dependent questions with AUC 0.93 at 7B. On five benchmarks spanning arithmetic, multi-hop factual QA, and numerical reasoning over financial tables, CARL improves exact-match accuracy by 6.7 points at 7B and 9.7 points at 3B over the best RL baseline, with the largest gain (+8.3 EM at 7B, +9.0 EM at 3B) on Musique. The model issues 53\% fewer tool calls on parametrically answerable questions while remaining ${\sim}10$ EM points more accurate on them. Gains are largest at small scale: the 3B improvement is $1.4\times$ the 7B improvement, suggesting that knowing when to ask disproportionately benefits models with smaller parametric memory.

</details>


### [212] [SYNAPSE: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Thought-to-Text Decoding via Topological Semantic Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27790)
*Akshaj Murhekar,Abhijit Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SYNAPSE框架，通过符号正则化提升EEG到文本解码的语义稳定性，无需微调LLM即可达到与资源密集系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG到文本解码系统易受生物噪声影响，导致冻结语言模型产生幻觉或不稳定的语义生成，亟需轻量级解决方案提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SYNAPSE神经符号框架，利用常识图结构和潜在样本对EEG语义候选进行推理时符号正则化，净化神经投影，无需端到端LLM微调。

Result: 在多个EEG解码基准和冻结LLM后端上持续优于无约束基线，在目标标签消融下表现鲁棒，性能与资源密集的微调系统相当。

Conclusion: SYNAPSE通过轻量符号正则化有效提升了神经文本生成的语义稳定性，同时通过将原始EEG处理限制在编码器栈内保护了生物特征隐私。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated open-vocabulary EEG-to-imagined-text decoding, where non-invasive neural activity recorded during visual perception is translated into coherent natural language descriptions of viewed stimuli. However, existing systems remain highly vulnerable to biological noise, where corrupted neural projections induce hallucinated or semantically unstable generation in frozen language models. We introduce SYNAPSE (Symbolic Neural Alignment for Precise Semantic Extraction), a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework that stabilizes neural text generation through inference-time symbolic regularization. By purifying EEG-derived semantic candidates using commonsense graph structure and latent exemplars, SYNAPSE improves semantic stability without end-to-end LLM fine-tuning. Experiments across popular EEG decoding benchmarks and multiple frozen LLM backends demonstrate consistent gains over unconstrained prompting baselines, robustness under object-label ablation, and performance commensurate with substantially more resource-intensive fine-tuned systems, while preserving biometric privacy by localizing raw EEG processing entirely within the encoder stack.

</details>


### [213] [Density-aware Sample-specific Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27809)
*Qiyuan Wang,Yao Li,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于低密度区域引导的样本特定后门攻击方法，通过密度比估计和双层级优化，在多种防御下保持极高攻击成功率（防御后仍达50-85%），并完全免疫神经元剪枝防御，揭示了现有防御范式的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击方法容易受到微调或剪枝等防御手段的清除，缺乏对攻击核心目标的系统性分析，需要一种理论上更优、对防御更具鲁棒性的攻击方法。

Method: 通过贝叶斯最优训练模型推导出最优样本特定触发器的构造准则——将触发样本引导至干净数据分布的低密度区域；采用条件时间分数匹配估计密度比，构建双层级优化框架，优化混合模型目标函数以实现该分布条件。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上，防御前ASR超99%；微调防御下比最强基线高出50-85个百分点；神经元剪枝防御下完全免疫，所有剪枝阈值下均无神经元被识别移除。

Conclusion: 当前防御范式存在根本性缺陷，需要设计能超越干净数据分布支撑集进行操作的防御方法。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.

</details>


### [214] [ReSAE: Residualized Sparse Autoencoders for Multi-Layer Transformer Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27819)
*Prathyush Poduval,Calvin Yeung,Neel Desai,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差化稀疏自编码器（ReSAE），通过去除非线性耦合的线性可预测跨层结构，在多层级SAE替换中更好地保留下游计算相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAE逐层独立训练，忽略了Transformer残差流激活在深度上的强耦合，导致多层干预时字典容量冗余、替换行为不可预测。

Method: ReSAE在选定层之间拟合仿射映射，后续层SAE训练于残差（而非完整激活），重构时通过仿射链映射回原始激活空间，支持与普通SAE相同的干预协议。

Result: 在Pythia-1.4B和Gemma-2-9B上，残差化降低了解码器冗余，在稀疏探测和定向扰动中多数设置表现更优；虽重构更少原始方差，但在教师强制和足够稀疏条件下，多层替换恢复更多交叉熵。

Conclusion: 去除线性可预测的跨层结构作为多层SAE干预的默认方法是有益的。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are usually trained one layer at a time, even though transformer residual stream activations are strongly coupled across depth. This creates a practical problem for multi-layer interventions: different layerwise dictionaries can spend capacity representing the same carried-forward information, and replacing several layers at once can produce interactions that are not predicted by single-layer behavior. We introduce Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs), which fit an affine map between selected layers and train each later-layer SAE on the unexplained residual rather than on the full activation. Reconstructions are mapped back into the original activation space through the fitted affine chain, so ReSAEs can be evaluated with the same intervention protocols as ordinary SAEs. On Pythia-1.4B and Gemma-2-9B, residualization reduces decoder redundancy and improves sparse probing and targeted perturbation in most tested settings. Despite reconstructing less of the raw activation variance, ReSAEs recover more transformer cross entropy under multi-layer replacement. This gain is clearest under teacher-forcing and at sufficient sparsity online, indicating that ReSAEs preserve the components of the activation most relevant to the model's downstream computation. These results suggest that removing linearly predictable cross-layer structure is a useful default for multi-layer SAE interventions.

</details>


### [215] [Reward Transfer from Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A Coupled Minimax Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27834)
*Guang-Yuan Hao,Lars van der Laan,Aurélien Bibaut,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将在源环境中通过逆强化学习从专家演示中习得的奖励，迁移到目标环境中的强化学习问题。提出了联合求解源与目标环境的贝尔曼方程组方法（耦合方法），相比先估计奖励再求解的序贯方法，耦合方法能消除源环境贝尔曼残差误差的一阶影响，并提供了理论误差界和遗憾保证，通过败血症模拟器验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当专家演示在受控环境（源环境）中收集时，直接在不同环境（目标环境）中使用这些奖励会导致性能下降。现有序贯方法先估计源奖励再用于目标控制，会积累误差。因此需要一种能联合考虑源与目标环境的方法来减少误差传播。

Method: 提出耦合方法，联合求解源和目标环境的贝尔曼方程组，构建Minimax估计器估计目标soft-q函数。与序贯方法（先估计源奖励再插入目标控制问题）对比，从理论上分析两者差异，并推导有限样本误差界和遗憾界。

Result: 理论结果表明耦合方法能够消除源环境贝尔曼残差误差的一阶影响，而序贯方法则无法避免。通过败血症模拟器的实证研究验证了理论对比结论。

Conclusion: 耦合方法在迁移逆强化学习奖励到新环境时优于序贯方法，能显著降低误差传播，具有更好的理论保障和实际表现。

Abstract: We study the transfer of rewards learned using inverse reinforcement learning from expert demonstrations in one environment to reinforcement learning in a new, different environment. This arises naturally when demonstrations are collected in a controlled environment. We formulate the problem as a joint system of Bellman equations across the source and target environments and develop minimax estimators for the target soft-$q$-function. Whereas a sequential solution approach first estimates the source reward and then plugs it into the target control problem, a coupled approach solves the source and target system of equations jointly. We show that, in contrast to the sequential approach, the coupled approach removes the first-order influence of source Bellman residual error. We characterize the local behavior of each approach, develop finite-sample soft-$q$-function error bounds, and prove regret guarantees for the resulting soft-control policy. An empirical investigation using a sepsis simulator validates the theoretical comparison.

</details>


### [216] [CAREF: Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness Without Rationale Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27835)
*Naphat Nithisopa,Teerapong Panboonyuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CAREF框架，通过校准感知正则化联合优化预测准确性和解释忠实度，在仅使用6.43%可训练参数的情况下，在四个NLE基准上优于LoRA和AdaLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）在提升模型性能的同时，往往牺牲了解释的忠实度（faithfulness），缺乏同时优化准确性和可解释性的统一框架。

Method: 提出CAREF框架，核心是LSCED损失函数，将基于熵的校准（entropy-based calibration）与token级稀疏控制（token-level sparsity control）耦合到单一训练目标中，无需理性（rationale）监督信号。

Result: 在COS-E、ECQA、ComVE、e-SNLI四个基准上，CAREF-AQ变体在Flan-T5上取得最佳平均准确率（89.04）和解释对齐度（81.00 nBERT），仅使用6.43%可训练参数，优于LoRA和AdaLoRA。

Conclusion: CAREF是首个将熵正则化与稀疏正则化统一到单一训练目标中的可解释LLM微调方法，为参数高效微调中的忠实解释生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce CAREF, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and explanation faithfulness via calibration-aware regularization. At its core, CAREF couples entropy-based calibration with token-level sparsity control through a single unified loss, the Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness (LSCED), without requiring rationale supervision. Evaluated on four NLE benchmarks (COS-E, ECQA, ComVE, e-SNLI) with Flan-T5, our lightweight CAREF-AQ variant attains the best average accuracy (89.04) and explanation alignment (81.00 nBERT) using only 6.43% of trainable parameters, outperforming LoRA and AdaLoRA. To our knowledge, CAREF is the first method to unify entropy and sparsity regularization in a single training objective for interpretable LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [217] [SPAR: Support-Preserving Action Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27877)
*Jiaxin Zhao,Weihang Pan,Xun Liang,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPAR通过将全局学习重构为局部残差校正，解决了离线策略改进中价值最大化与数据分布拟合之间的冲突，在D4RL基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略改进面临内在冲突：价值最大化与数据分布拟合之间的矛盾。现有方法要么过于保守（抑制分布尾部的高价值动作），要么存在梯度冲突（驱动策略偏离数据流形）。

Method: 提出支持保持动作校正（SPAR），将全局学习重构为基于冻结纯行为克隆策略的局部残差校正框架；引入潜在自模仿机制，通过潜在采样加权回归解决残差空间中的拟合-改进梯度冲突。

Result: 理论证明该方法消除了标准价值梯度的流形法向漂移；在D4RL实验上，SPAR从次优基线中提取显著收益，达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SPAR通过局部残差校正和潜在自模仿机制，有效缓解了离线策略改进中的过保守与梯度冲突问题，实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: Offline policy improvement faces an inherent conflict between maximizing value and fitting the data distribution. While in-sample weighted regression is stable, it suffers from over-conservatism that suppresses high-value actions in the distribution tail; conversely, gradient-based approaches often exhibit a fitting-optimization conflict of gradients, which drives the policy off the data manifold. To address this, we propose Support-Preserving Action Rectification (SPAR), which reframes global learning as a local residual rectification anchored to a frozen pure behavior cloning policy. This framework performs fine-grained fitting and local policy improvement in the residual space, thereby contracting the search space. We further introduce Latent Self-Imitation, utilizing a latent-sampling weighted-regression mechanism to address fitting-improvement gradient conflict in the residual space. Theoretically, we prove this mechanism eliminates the manifold-normal drift of standard value gradients, while extensive D4RL experiments show SPAR extracts significant gains from suboptimal baselines to achieve state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [218] [FedEHR-Gen: Federated Synthetic Time-Series EHR Generation via Latent Space Alignment and Distribution-Aware Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27892)
*Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedEHR-Gen，首个面向分布式医院的时间序列合成电子健康记录数据的联邦学习框架，通过两阶段学习范式解决高维、稀疏及跨医院异质性挑战，性能接近集中式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR生成模型多为集中式，需汇集多医院数据，但因隐私限制难以实现；直接联邦建模因EHR数据的高维性、稀疏性和跨医院异质性而容易崩溃或发散。

Method: 两阶段学习范式：(1) 联邦自编码器将高维稀疏EHR特征投影到紧凑潜空间，通过层匹配聚合机制保证语义一致性；(2) 在对齐的潜空间上训练联邦时间条件变分自编码器（TCVAE），采用分布感知聚合应对跨医院异质性。

Result: 在eICU和MIMIC-III数据集上，生成保真度、下游效用和隐私风险与集中式训练相当，且一致优于标准联邦基线方法。

Conclusion: FedEHR-Gen有效解决了隐私约束下的分布式EHR生成问题，为跨医院数据增强和建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Synthetic Electronic Health Record (EHR) generation provides a promising avenue for data augmentation and cross-hospital modeling in privacy-constrained healthcare settings. However, most existing EHR generative models are centralized and require pooling data across hospitals, which is often infeasible when real-world data sharing is restricted. While federated EHR generation offers a natural solution, direct federated modeling often collapses or diverges due to the high dimensionality, sparsity, and cross-hospital heterogeneity of EHR data. In this work, we propose FedEHR-Gen, the first federated framework for synthetic time-series EHR generation across distributed hospitals. FedEHR-Gen uses a two-stage learning paradigm. First, we introduce a federated autoencoder that projects high-dimensional and sparse EHR features onto a compact latent space. To ensure semantic consistency across hospitals, we develop a layer-wise matching aggregation mechanism that aligns local encoders into a unified global latent space. Second, operating on this aligned latent space, we train a federated temporal conditional variational autoencoder (TCVAE) with distribution-aware aggregation, enabling stable temporal generative modeling under severe cross-hospital heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on the eICU and MIMIC-III datasets demonstrate that FedEHR-Gen achieves generation fidelity, downstream utility, and privacy risk comparable to centralized training, while consistently outperforming the standard federated baseline.

</details>


### [219] [Cyclical Entropy Eruption: Entropy Dynamics in Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27954)
*Wendi Li,Shawn Im,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了智能体强化学习中一种名为“循环熵爆发”的新现象，并提出了SEAL方法来稳定训练、提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习的训练动态尚不明确，限制了诊断不稳定性和设计更优训练算法的能力，因此需要深入理解其内在机制。

Method: 识别出循环熵爆发的三阶段动态过程，并进行理论与实证分析；提出SEAL（分离增强智能体学习）轻量级辅助损失，在表征空间中分离正确与错误轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，SEAL能在多个基准、模型和RL算法上稳定训练，并提升下游智能体任务性能。

Conclusion: 循环熵爆发是智能体RL中的特有现象，会导致退化模式累积；SEAL通过表征分离有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Agentic large language models are increasingly used to solve real-world tasks by reasoning over goals, invoking tools, and interacting with external environments. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for improving these behaviors, and recent agent RL methods have achieved strong results across domains. However, the training dynamics of agent RL remain poorly understood, limiting our ability to diagnose instabilities and design more effective training algorithms. In this work, we identify a previously underexplored phenomenon in agent RL, which we term cyclical entropy eruption. Unlike single-turn reasoning RL, where entropy typically collapses and stays low, agent RL training exhibits unique recurring cycles of sharp entropy eruption and gradual subsidence. We decompose this dynamic into three phases and provide theoretical and empirical analyses of each, explaining the mechanisms underlying its cyclical oscillation. We further show that degenerate patterns such as sentence duplication and hallucination, once acquired during eruption, can persist and accumulate across cycles. Motivated by these findings, we propose SEAL (Separation-Enhanced Agent Learning), a lightweight auxiliary loss that separates correct and incorrect trajectories in representation space, directly targeting the root cause of entropy eruption. Experiments across multiple benchmarks, models, and RL algorithms demonstrate that SEAL stabilizes training and yields stronger downstream agent performance.

</details>


### [220] [Law of Neural Interaction: Depth-Width Shape, Interaction Efficiency, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27989)
*Wenjie Sun,Jinning Yang,Shuai Zhang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在固定预算下的资源利用效率与泛化能力相关，提出“神经交互”概念，并发现通过调整深度-宽度比可将模型置于高效交互区间，该区间随预算扩大保持稳定，且在该区间附近的模型在MMLU-Pro上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在缩放定律指导下资源需求日益增长，但固定预算下资源利用效率是否最优尚不清楚。前人已证明叠加态是损失的重要来源，本文希望进一步探索资源利用效率与泛化之间的关系。

Method: 利用神经特征拟设（Neural Feature Ansatz），将叠加态从参数空间扩展到梯度空间，定义为“神经交互”。通过调整模型的深度-宽度比，观察模型在不同交互效率下的表现。

Result: 固定预算下，良好的泛化通常伴随着高效的神经交互；通过调整深度-宽度比可将模型置于高效交互区间；随着预算扩大，该区间保持相对稳定；在该区间附近的小规模稠密LLM在MMLU-Pro上表现更好。

Conclusion: 深度-宽度比影响资源利用效率，进而影响泛化能力，为模型形状初始化和理解模型泛化机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: The guidance of scaling laws has increased the resource demands of modern large language models (LLMs), yet it remains questionable whether these models utilize resources effectively under a fixed budget. Previous research has proved superposition as a key contributor to loss. By leveraging the Neural Feature Ansatz, we extend superposition from parameter space to gradient space and define it as neural interaction. We find that under a fixed budget, good generalization is usually accompanied by efficient neural interactions, and the model can be placed in an efficient interaction interval by adjusting its depth-width ratio ($R_{D/W}$). In addition, as the budget scales up, the efficient interaction interval of the model remains relatively stable. By comparing existing small scale dense LLMs, we observe that models operating near this interval tend to perform better on the MMLU-Pro benchmark. Our findings reveal that the $R_{D/W}$ influences resource utilization efficiency and thereby affects generalization, providing insights into model shape initialization and the understanding of model generalization mechanisms. Code for Neural Interaction Law is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Neural_Interaction_Law-D788

</details>


### [221] [Geometry-Correct Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Denoiser-Pullback Curvature Guidance and Manifold-Aligned Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27990)
*Seunghyeok Shin,Minwoo Kim,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于阻尼高斯-牛顿校正的扩散后验采样方法，用每噪声级别的校正代替传统的手动调节指导权重，在多个逆问题任务上取得了有竞争力的结果，并在加速MRI重建中达到了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散后验采样中，数据一致性更新依赖于手动调节的指导权重，且在不同算子作用下容易导致采样不稳定。

Method: 提出每噪声级别的阻尼高斯-牛顿校正，在扩散状态坐标中进行计算；通过单边曲率模型避免前向去噪器雅可比矩阵；使用扩散校准的秩一阻尼；通过自动微分的无矩阵GMRES求解校正；采用具有闭式漂移/噪声分裂的方差保持朗之万转移进行采样。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet的逆问题任务中，取得了有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS指标，且运行速度显著快于大多数对比基线；在加速MRI重建中，达到了对比基线中的最佳PSNR/SSIM。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了传统扩散后验采样中指导权重手动调节和采样不稳定问题，在多个任务上表现优异，尤其在MRI重建中效果突出。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior sampling conditions diffusion priors on measurements, but data-consistency updates are typically scaled by hand-tuned guidance weights and can destabilize sampling under stiff, operator-dependent curvature. We replace scalar guidance with a per-noise-level damped Gauss--Newton correction computed in diffusion-state coordinates. The correction pulls likelihood gradients back through the denoiser, uses a one-sided curvature model that avoids forward denoiser Jacobians, and applies diffusion-calibrated rank-one damping aligned with the denoiser residual. Each correction is solved with matrix-free GMRES using automatic differentiation, and sampling proceeds with a variance-preserving Langevin transition with a closed-form drift/noise split. On FFHQ and ImageNet across inverse problems, it achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS while running markedly faster than most of the compared baselines; on accelerated MRI reconstruction, it achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared baselines.

</details>


### [222] [AOE: Exhaustive Out-of-Distribution Detection via Recalibrating Outlier Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28021)
*Fengqiang Wan,Qing-Yuan Jiang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应置信度异常暴露（AOE）方法，通过温度缩放为OOD样本生成自适应软标签，在保留OOD与ID类别语义关系的同时扩大分离边界，从而提升OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Outlier Exposure的方法使用均匀标签来最大化OOD样本的熵，但理论上证明均匀标签会忽略OOD样本与ID类别之间的关系（过度软化效应），导致次优的边界。

Method: 提出自适应置信度异常暴露（AOE），利用温度缩放对异常标签进行重新校准。具体地，AOE从温度缩放的模型预测中为OOD样本生成自适应软目标，可学习的温度在平滑预测分布的同时不完全消除类别间的关联信息。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了AOE方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过自适应软目标监督OOD样本，既保留了OOD样本与ID类别的语义邻近性，又鼓励软化目标接近高熵分布，从而抑制过度自信的OOD预测并扩大分离边界。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for deploying machine learning models in open-world and safety-critical scenarios, where test inputs may deviate from the training distribution and overconfident predictions on unknown samples can lead to unreliable decisions. Outlier Exposure (OE) has emerged as a promising OOD detection paradigm by introducing auxiliary outliers during training to enlarge the margin between in-distribution (ID) and OOD samples. Existing OE-based methods typically enlarge this margin by employing uniform labels to maximize the entropy of OOD samples over ID categories. However, we theoretically show that uniform labels inevitably disregard the relations between OOD samples and ID categories, termed the over-softening effect, leading to a suboptimal margin bound. Our theoretical analysis further reveals that explicitly exploiting such relations can instead yield improved OOD detection performance. Motivated by this insight, we propose \underline{A}daptive Confidence \underline{OE} (AOE), a simple yet effective method that leverages temperature scaling to recalibrate outlier labels. Specifically, AOE generates adaptive soft targets from temperature-scaled model predictions for OOD samples, where the learnable temperature smooths the prediction distribution without fully erasing class-wise relational information. By supervising OOD samples with these adaptive soft targets, AOE preserves the semantic proximity between OOD samples and ID categories while encouraging the softened targets to approach a high-entropy distribution, thereby suppressing overconfident OOD predictions and enlarging the separation margin. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of AOE.

</details>


### [223] [PINE: Pruning Boosted Tree Ensembles with Conformal In-Distribution Prediction Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28068)
*Haruki Yajima,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINE是一种针对树集成模型的剪枝方法，通过在分布内区域保持预测等价性，并利用保形校准控制区域大小，在12个表格数据集上实现了比现有忠实剪枝方法最高30%的压缩率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的忠实剪枝方法虽然能保证在整个输入空间上的预测等价性，但压缩率较低；而标准剪枝方法可能改变部分预测结果，影响决策一致性。作者希望在保证预测一致性的前提下，提高压缩率。

Method: 提出了PINE方法，通过保形校准（conformal calibration）引入一个参数α来控制分布内区域的大小，在该区域内保持预测等价性，区域外则不做保证。

Result: 在12个公开表格数据集上，PINE相比现有忠实剪枝方法，压缩率最高提升了30%，同时预测保持水平相当。

Conclusion: PINE在预测一致性和压缩率之间取得了更好的平衡，通过松弛全局等价性要求为分布内区域的强保证，显著提升了剪枝效率。

Abstract: Tree ensembles are machine learning models with strong predictive performance and interpretability, and remain widely used for tabular data. Standard pruning methods for tree ensembles typically optimize an accuracy-compression trade-off and may change a subset of predictions, potentially compromising decision consistency. Faithful pruning methods address this issue by preserving prediction equivalence over the entire input space, but this requirement leads to lower compression ratios. We propose PINE, a pruning method that provides strong guarantees within an in-distribution region. PINE preserves prediction equivalence within this region and controls the region size using a single parameter $α$ via conformal calibration. Experiments on 12 public tabular datasets show that PINE improves the compression ratio by up to $30\%$ while preserving predictions at a comparable level to existing faithful pruning methods.

</details>


### [224] [Measure-to-measure Regression with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28075)
*Matthew Vandergrift,Martha White,Yury Polyanskiy,Philippe Rigollet,Lazar Atanackovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量到度量（M2M）回归问题，即从有限观测的输入-输出对中学习概率测度之间的映射。作者提出了两种基于Transformer的解决方案：静态M2M映射和动态M2M速度场，并在合成实验、相互作用粒子系统以及结直肠癌类器官数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学应用（如细胞生物学）需要预测群体在未知变换下的演化，传统回归将样本视为独立个体而忽略了群体分布的整体结构，因此需要一种能够处理概率测度之间映射的回归方法。

Method: 提出了非线性M2M回归的形式化框架，并设计了两种基于Transformer的方法：1) 静态M2M映射，直接学习测度间的变换；2) 动态M2M速度场，学习测度演化的速度场。利用Transformer天然的度量依赖性和平均场结构来学习概率分布空间上的非线性映射。

Result: 在合成实验、相互作用粒子系统和大规模患者来源的结直肠癌类器官数据集中，所提方法能够有效泛化到未见过的测度，成功预测了治疗反应。

Conclusion: 提出的基于Transformer的M2M回归方法兼具表达能力、易用性和可扩展性，为群体演化预测提供了新范式，尤其是在生命科学领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Many learning problems require predicting how populations evolve under an unknown transformation. A natural representation for such populations is a probability measure, with point clouds as a key example. In this work, we study the measure-to-measure (M2M) regression problem, in which one seeks to learn a map between probability measures from a finite collection of observed input-output pairs. In contrast to classical regression, where individual samples are transformed independently, M2M regression treats entire distributions as the data points. This perspective is vital in certain scientific applications, for example, cellular and molecular biology, where cells are known to evolve not as independent data points but as a collection. However, few existing approaches address the problem of M2M regression with sufficient expressivity and scalability. We present a formalization of nonlinear M2M regression and introduce two easy-to-use, expressive, and scalable approaches to learn such operators: transformers as static M2M maps and transformers as dynamic M2M velocity fields. Our approach leverages the natural measure-dependent and mean-field structure of transformers to learn nonlinear M2M maps on the space of probability distributions. We illustrate the effectiveness of our proposed method to generalize to unseen measures on synthetic experiments, interacting particle systems, and a large-scale patient-derived organoid dataset for predicting treatment response in colorectal cancer.

</details>


### [225] [Long Live The Balance: Information Bottleneck Driven Tree-based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28109)
*Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Tianpeng Bu,Bowen Xu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hongtao Duan,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息瓶颈理论的IB-Score指标和IB-TPO框架，通过平衡探索与利用来优化大语言模型的在线强化学习，在多个基准上超越GRPO等方法2.9%-3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线RL方法在LLM推理任务中探索-利用平衡不佳，导致优化不稳定和次优性能。

Method: 提出IB-Score指标量化探索-利用平衡，并设计IB-TPO框架，包含IB引导的树采样策略和IB-Score蒙特卡洛估计，在相同token预算下多生成50%轨迹。

Result: 在标准基准上，IB-TPO相比GRPO提升2.9%-3.6%，并超越其他SOTA在线RL方法，采样效率提升50%。

Conclusion: IB-Score和IB-TPO有效解决了在线RL中的探索-利用失衡问题，为LLM在线强化学习提供了更稳定的优化框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in online reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising performance in complex reasoning tasks. However, they often exhibit an imbalanced exploration-exploitation trade-off, resulting in unstable optimization and sub-optimal performance. We introduce IB-Score, a novel metric grounded in Information Bottleneck theory that evaluates policy's exploration-exploitation balance by quantifying the trade-off between step-level reasoning diversity and mutual information shared with the correct answer. Analysis based on IB-Score shows that popular online RL approaches (e.g., GRPO) with common regularizers fail to consistently maintain balance during training with suboptimal results. To address this, we propose Information Bottleneck-driven Tree-based Policy Optimization (IB-TPO), a principled framework that formulates IB-Score as a fine-grained optimization objective and utilizes a novel IB-guided tree sampling strategy that not only improves the efficiency of online sampling with 50% more trajectories under the same token budget, but also reuses the tree structure for effective IB-Score Monte Carlo estimation. Extensive experiments across standard benchmarks show that our method significantly outperforms GRPO baseline by 2.9% to 3.6% and also outperforms other state-of-the-art online RL approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/alibaba/EfficientRL.

</details>


### [226] [Adaptive Coarse-to-Fine Subgoal Refinement for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28127)
*Kaiqiang Ke,Shenghong He,Chengdong Xu,Yuheng Luo,Xiangyuan Lan,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CFHRL，一种全离线分层目标强化学习框架，通过自适应递归细化遥远目标，并在可学习到达代价判定局部可执行时停止细化，有效解决长程任务中远距离目标监督弱和值估计误差累积的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL在长程任务中面临远距离状态-目标对监督信号弱和自举误差累积的挑战；现有分层方法使用固定时间抽象或固定层次深度，无法适应不同可达性距离的状态-目标对。

Method: 从最终目标开始递归提出中间子目标，子目标从回放支持候选中训练生成，通过学习到达代价估计当前目标是否局部可执行来决定停止细化。子目标不要求精确中点，只要求提供可靠进展并降低后续到达难度。

Result: 在OGBench多个长程任务上取得了显著提升，消融实验验证了所提细化机制和停止机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 近似递归收缩的鲁棒性在理论分析中得到支持，证明了自适应细化框架在离线长程GCRL中的有效性。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) is challenging in long-horizon tasks, where distant state--goal pairs provide weak supervision and value estimates become vulnerable to accumulated bootstrapping errors. Hierarchical methods mitigate this difficulty by introducing intermediate subgoals, but fixed temporal abstractions or fixed hierarchy depths can be mismatched to state--goal pairs with different reachability horizons. We propose Coarse-to-Fine Hierarchical Goal Reinforcement Learning (CFHRL), a fully offline GCRL framework that adaptively refines distant goals before execution. Starting from the final goal, CFHRL recursively proposes intermediate targets, trained from replay-supported candidates, and stops refinement once the current target is estimated to be locally executable by a learned reachability cost. The key idea is that a subgoal need not be an exact midpoint or globally optimal waypoint; it only needs to provide reliable progress and reduce the remaining reaching difficulty, enabling subsequent refinement over shorter horizons. A stylized analysis further supports the robustness of approximate recursive contraction. Experiments on OGBench show substantial gains on several long-horizon tasks, with ablations validating the proposed refinement and stopping mechanisms

</details>


### [227] [Learning to Bid in Repeated Second-Price Auctions with Dynamic Values and Aggregated Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28133)
*Benjamin Heymann,Otmane Sakhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究投标者在动态价值（依赖于过去中标结果）下的学习投标问题，提出结合插件估计与微分方程最优策略的学习方法，在分段线性原始变量下实现近最优的$\widetilde{O}(\log N)$遗憾，在一般光滑原始变量下实现$\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实投标场景中，投标者的价值往往动态变化（如依赖于上次中标后的时间间隔），现有研究多假设价值静态，因此需要研究动态价值下的学习投标问题。

Method: 结合插件估计器与微分方程刻画最优策略，设计了置信边界算法，无需显式随机化即可实现学习。

Result: 分段线性原始变量下遗憾界为$\widetilde{O}(\log N)$，一般光滑原始变量下为$\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$，均达到近最优。

Conclusion: 所提方法在动态价值投标问题中有效，理论结果与数值实验一致。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning to bid when the bidder's value is dynamic, i.e., when the current value depends on past outcomes. Specifically, we consider a bidder participating in repeated second-price auctions whose value depends on the time elapsed since their last successful bid, with auctions arriving in continuous time and only aggregated feedback revealed at the end of the horizon. Such a bidder must (1) balance the immediate benefit of winning the current auction against its impact on future values and (2) learn unknown environmental parameters. We derive regret bounds for a class of learning methods that combine plug-in estimators with a differential-equation characterization of the optimal policy, and show that a specific confidence bound algorithm learns the optimal policy with a near optimal regret of $\widetilde{O}(\log N)$ for piecewise linear primitives, and $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ for general, smooth primitives, achieving these regrets without explicit randomization. These theoretical results are supported by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [228] [Self-Consistency via Marginal Sharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28142)
*Aleksei Arzhantsev,Otmane Sakhi,Nicolas Chopin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理时采样的目标从完整输出分布转向锐化的答案边际分布，通过一种自回归并行采样算法近似地从锐化答案边际中采样，在数学和编程基准上比标准power采样性能更强且速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有power采样方法通过锐化完整生成输出的分布来提升推理能力，但该方法将推理轨迹与最终答案纠缠在一起，而实际上重要的是一个答案是否被多条合理的推理路径所支持，因此需要将目标从完整输出分布转向答案边际分布。

Method: 提出将自一致性（self-consistency）从后验投票准则转变为推理时的优化目标，设计了一种纯自回归的并行采样算法，高效地近似从锐化答案边际中采样。

Result: 在数学和编程基准测试上，该方法比标准power采样表现更强，同时速度提升了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 推理时应当关注答案的边际概率而非完整输出概率，自一致性能作为推理时的目标高效近似地实现这一目的。

Abstract: Inference-time sampling can elicit strong reasoning abilities from language models without additional training. Existing power-sampling methods do so by sharpening the distribution over full generated outputs, favoring completions that are individually likely under the model. We argue that this is the wrong object to target for reasoning: a completion entangles a reasoning trace with a final answer, whereas what matters is whether an answer is supported by many plausible reasoning paths. We therefore shift the target from the full-output distribution to the sharpened answer marginal, making self-consistency an inference-time objective rather than a post-hoc voting criterion. Surprisingly, this marginal target admits an efficient approximation: we propose a simple, purely autoregressive parallel sampling algorithm that approximately samples from the sharpened answer marginal, eliciting stronger performance than standard power sampling on mathematics and coding benchmarks while being orders of magnitude faster.

</details>


### [229] [Sequential Neural Probabilistic Amplitude Shaping: Learning the Channel's Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28143)
*Mohammad Taha Askari,Lutz Lampe,Amirhossein Ghazisaeidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能超越现有方法且考虑所有实现损失的神经概率幅度成形方案，采用无块、易于实现的序列自回归编码器与算术分布匹配，降低了速率损失并提高了可达信息速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率幅度成形方法存在实现损失考虑不足、速率损失较大等问题，亟需一种更高效、更实用的方案。

Method: 采用无块结构的序列自回归编码器，结合算术分布匹配技术，实现神经概率幅度成形。

Result: 所提方法在考虑所有实现损失的情况下，仍能超越现有方法，降低了速率损失，并提高了可达信息速率。

Conclusion: 该神经概率幅度成形方案在实用性和性能上均优于现有方法，具有良好的可实施性和高效性。

Abstract: We present the first neural probabilistic amplitude shaping that outperforms existing methods while accounting for all implementation losses, using a block-less, easily implementable sequential autoregressive encoder compatible with arithmetic distribution matching, yielding reduced rate loss and higher achievable information rates.

</details>


### [230] [Sign-Aware Gated Sparse Autoencoders: Modeling Anticorrelated Features with Bi-Jump-ReLU Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28149)
*Bartosz Wieciech,Zmnako Awrahman,Marcin Czelej,Victor Hugo Jaramillo Velasquez,Wioletta Stobieniecka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sign-Aware Gated SAE (SA-GSAE)，通过双面门控稀疏性和符号感知机制，让单个潜变量同时编码正负极性概念，提升字典效率并大幅降低死神经元比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器强制非负性，导致极性相反的概念（如"压力过高"vs"压力过低"）需要使用不同潜变量，浪费字典容量。

Method: 提出SA-GSAE，包含：极性敏感门控（双面选择）、符号幅度路径（避免L1收缩）、辅助重建损失（防止门控崩溃），以及Bi-Jump-ReLU激活函数实现双极性共享。

Result: 在Pythia-1B和SmolLM3-3B上，半宽SA-GSAE在3/6个细胞点上严格Pareto优于全宽Gated SAE；死神经元比例降低0.35-0.62绝对值；几何平均死神经元减少约100-500倍。

Conclusion: 双面门控和辅助损失是关键组件；推荐对称变体（r_i^+ = r_i^-）作为默认配置；半宽版本能完全避免全宽版本在SmolLM3-3B残差上的重建崩溃问题。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) extract interpretable features from Large Language Models, but standard variants enforce non-negativity, forcing separate latents for diametrically opposed concepts (e.g., "pressure too high" vs. "pressure too low") and wasting dictionary capacity when features are anticorrelated. We propose the Sign-Aware Gated SAE (SA-GSAE): two-sided gated sparsity with signed magnitude and auxiliary supervision. A polarity-sensitive gate selects support on either sign, a signed-magnitude path avoids L1 shrinkage, and an auxiliary reconstruction prevents gate collapse. Bipolar sharing - one latent encoding both signs along a shared direction - is realised via a new Bi-Jump-ReLU activation; parameter accounting shows sign-awareness stays parameter-efficient even when anticorrelated pairs are rare.
  On real LLM activations across three mid-depth hookpoints on Pythia-1B and SmolLM3-3B (6 cells, 3 seeds), a half-width SA-GSAE at width H strictly Pareto-dominates a full-width Gated SAE at 2H over the entire swept L0 overlap on 3 of 6 cells (both MLP-output hookpoints and resid-mid/Pythia-1B); on the remaining 3 it matches R^2 within 0.025 (max gap -0.008) while cutting dead fraction by 0.35-0.62 absolute. Sweep-geomean dead-fraction reductions are ~100x-500x on MLP-output cells and Pythia-1B resid, ~2x-4x on attention cells and SmolLM3-3B resid. Ablations show the two-sided gate and auxiliary loss are load-bearing (no auxiliary collapses LR to 0.27, 98% dead); tying r_i^+ = r_i^- is indistinguishable (|Delta R^2| = 0.0015), and we recommend this symmetric variant as default. MLP-output gains come from most latents carrying both polarities; on attention, bipolar structure concentrates in a small set of top latents. Full-width SA-GSAE exhibits a reproducible reconstruction collapse at SmolLM3-3B resid that the half-width entirely avoids.

</details>


### [231] [Off-Policy Learning to Reason Works Because It Is More Pessimistic Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28150)
*Otmane Sakhi,Aleksei Arzhantsev,Imad Aouali,Flavian Vasile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大规模强化学习中离线策略（off-policy）训练的问题，指出现有方法（如PPO）使用重要性权重进行分布校正会引入高方差和不稳定性。作者提出一种隐式悲观主义（implicit pessimism）视角，解释为何去除重要性权重的离线策略目标函数效果更好，并提出了稳定隐式目标分布的原则性改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的关键工具，但异步训练导致策略更新基于旧策略收集的数据（离线策略学习）。现有PPO风格方法使用重要性权重校正分布偏差，却带来高方差、不稳定性和熵坍缩问题，亟需更有效的离线策略训练方法。

Method: 作者从隐式悲观主义视角出发，构建了不依赖重要性权重的离线策略目标函数，分析了其有效性源于对目标策略的保守性优化。在此基础上，提出一种原则性的改进方法，以稳定隐式目标分布并提升离线学习效果。

Result: 隐式悲观主义视角成功解释了去除重要性权重的离线策略目标为何有效——它们隐式地优化向比名义目标更保守的策略。基于此理解，提出的改进方法能够稳定隐式目标分布，提升离线学习的稳定性。

Conclusion: 论文为离线策略强化学习提供了新的理论视角（隐式悲观主义），阐明了去除重要性权重方法的成功机理，并为设计更稳定的离线策略学习算法指明了方向。

Abstract: Large scale reinforcement learning has become a central tool for improving reasoning in large language models. At this scale, generation is often lagged or asynchronous, so updates are performed on data collected by older policies. This makes learning inherently off-policy. Most existing approaches nevertheless remain rooted in PPO-style trust-region objectives, treating training as approximately on-policy and using importance weights to correct distribution mismatch. These corrections can introduce high variance, destabilize optimization, and accelerate entropy collapse. Recent work suggests an alternative: rather than correcting the mismatch, one can embrace off-policy data and remove importance weights, often yielding stronger algorithms. In this paper, we provide an intuitive construction of off-policy objectives that include successful off-policy objectives and show that their effectiveness can be understood through implicit pessimism: they optimize toward target policies that are more conservative than their nominal objectives suggest. This perspective explains why some particular implementation choices improve stability: they implicitly control the effective target distribution. We then propose a principled modification that stabilize this induced distribution and improve off-policy learning.

</details>


### [232] [Unification and Optimization of Robust Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28165)
*Jonas Hanselle,Valentin Margraf,Clemens Damke,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将分布鲁棒优化、标签平滑、邻域风险最小化、Mixup等鲁棒学习方法组织到三个共同的设计轴上，通过分解为参考分布丰富、输入空间扰动、标签空间扰动和样本级聚合四个顺序阶段，每个阶段可选择悲观、中性或乐观三种立场，实现了对多种鲁棒策略的联合超参数优化，在表格、图像和奖励建模任务中与最佳单一方法性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习方法各自独立开发，迫使实践者必须事先承诺应对单一故障模式（如分布偏移、标签噪声、有限样本退化），而实际任务中主导故障模式往往不明确，缺乏一个能够组合和配置多种鲁棒策略的统一框架。

Method: 将现有方法组织到三个公共设计轴上，推导出一个可扩展的训练过程，将鲁棒学习分解为四个顺序阶段（参考分布丰富、输入空间扰动、标签空间扰动、样本级聚合），每个阶段支持悲观、中性或乐观三种立场，通过联合超参数优化来组合和配置适合当前任务的鲁棒策略。

Result: 在表格数据、图像数据和奖励建模三种基准测试中，联合超参数优化方法与每个设定下最佳单一方法基线性能相当，为事先不清楚主导故障模式的实践者提供了可靠的默认选择。

Conclusion: 统一的鲁棒学习设计空间使得在不预先知道任务主导故障模式的情况下，通过联合超参优化即可自动组合出适合的鲁棒策略，有望成为实践中的可靠默认方法。

Abstract: The literature has proposed various robust alternatives to empirical risk minimisation to address failure modes such as distribution shift, label noise and finite-sample degeneracies. Examples include distributionally robust optimization, label smoothing, vicinal risk minimization, and Mixup. However, such approaches are typically developed in isolation, forcing practitioners to commit a priori to a single failure mode even when the dominant mode for the task is unclear. To address this, we organize a broad class of existing methods along three common design axes and derive a tractable training procedure that decomposes robust learning into sequential stages (reference distribution enrichment, input-space perturbation, label-space perturbation, and sample-level aggregation), each with a choice of stance (pessimistic, neutral, or optimistic). This results in a unified design space in which joint hyperparameter optimization can compose and configure robustness strategies suited to the task at hand.
